On this natural elastane T-shirt, the round neck keeps the neckline simple. For this natural elastane T-shirt, short sleeve, a pull-on opening, and a straight hem complete the shape without adding fuss. For this natural elastane T-shirt, with no pockets to interrupt the front, the natural colour and solid finish do the work.
